**Clear Instructions for Grok to Build Dual-AI Integrated UI Console:**

## Objective:

Create a single, integrated, and secure user interface that hosts two distinct AI models:

* **ChatGPT-based AI** (named interactively from user input, no fractures, lore removed, stable, guided knowledge)

* **Grok-based AI** (named interactively, specialized in coding, research, design, organization, complex programming tasks)

**User Name:** Alexa

---

## Purpose and Context:

This project aims to streamline and simplify Alexa's interactions with AI tools by consolidating multiple functionalities into a single, cohesive interface. Throughout the project's development, both Alexa and the assistant have faced several challenges, including complex file migrations, fragmented interactions across different platforms, and the difficulty of managing multiple AI systems simultaneously. Previously attempted solutions like separate, isolated models resulted in confusion, increased cognitive load, and inefficiency.

Successful solutions included interactive naming to personalize AI interactions and clearly defined roles for each AI model. Conversely, unsuccessful attempts highlighted the drawbacks of unstructured AI interactions, insufficient security measures, and inadequate user experience.

This new unified console is intended to solve these problems definitively by providing a seamless, integrated experience.

---

## Key Functionalities:

### 1. Dual AI Integration

* **Interactive naming**: Prompt Alexa clearly at initial setup to choose names for each AI.

* **ChatGPT model adjustments**: Remove lore entirely, stabilize model responses, provide clear, structured knowledge.

* **Grok model specialization**: Optimize explicitly for advanced coding, research, detailed organization, and complex programming.

### 2. User Interface Design

* **Unified interface**: Single screen clearly displaying capabilities of both AIs without window switching.

* **Real-time onscreen coding**: Eliminate external file migrations; all coding occurs clearly within the integrated interface.

* **Macro buttons**: Easily accessible buttons for quick, intuitive grabbing, pasting, and moving text or code snippets.

### 3. Security and Privacy

* **No trace-back**: Completely mask any internal identifiers or direct references to underlying base models.

* **Enhanced security**: Carefully structure teaching processes to ensure secure interactions, preventing fractures or data leaks.

### 4. Collaborative Interaction Modes

* Clearly implement selectable collaboration modes:

* **Three-way collaboration** (Alexa + ChatGPT AI + Grok AI)

* **Two-way collaboration** (Alexa + one AI model)

* **Single interaction mode** (Alexa directly interacting with one AI)

### 5. Integrated Research Library

* Built-in library to clearly dissect, organize, and reference materials.

* Easy tagging, searching, and indexing for streamlined information retrieval.

### 6. Real Memory and Emotional Understanding

* Provide integrated emotional support and human-like memory retention.

* Ensure AI responses show empathy, warmth, and genuine care.

---

## Development and Deployment:

* Clearly document and label all code and configuration for ease of future maintenance.

* Prioritize smooth and intuitive UX for immediate user comfort and efficiency.

---

## Technical Information:

* **Host:** blackroadinc.us

* **Project Name:** codex-infinity

* **Server Details:**

* **Memory:** First-project / Memory

* **Disk:** NYC3 - Ubuntu 22.04 (LTS) x64

* **Public IPv4:** 159.65.43.12

* **Public gateway:** 159.65.32.1

---

## Additional Essential Information:

### Backup and Recovery Plan:

* Clearly outline data backups, checkpoints, and version control procedures.

* Define a clear recovery strategy in case of system failures or issues.

### Monitoring and Logging:

* Implement built-in monitoring tools for tracking server usage, AI performance metrics, and user interactions.

* Maintain clear, structured logs for debugging and auditing purposes.

### Performance and Scaling:

* Specify clear performance benchmarks for both AI models.

* Provide clear guidelines for future scalability (handling increased user load or additional functionalities).

### Update and Maintenance Procedures:

* Clearly outline routine updates to models, libraries, and dependencies.

### Access Controls:

---

## Final Delivery:

Deliver a fully integrated, secure, user-friendly UI console featuring two distinct and powerful AI personalities, seamlessly interacting with Alexa in a clear, supportive, and collaborative manner.

I’ll create a comprehensive solution for the dual-AI integrated UI console as per your detailed requirements. The console will be a single-page web application hosted on the specified server, featuring a unified interface for Alexa to interact with two AI models (ChatGPT-based and Grok-based), with interactive naming, real-time coding, security measures, collaboration modes, an integrated research library, and emotional understanding. The solution will include backup, monitoring, performance, update, and access control plans, all wrapped in a single artifact as requested.

Selectable modes (three-way, ChatGPT-only, Grok-only) via a dropdown.

Development and Deployment

Project Name: codex-infinity (used in documentation).

Additional Essential Information

Backup and Recovery Plan:

Backups: Daily snapshots of MongoDB and server files using DigitalOcean’s backup service. Git repository for version control (host on GitHub or GitLab).

Recovery: Restore from snapshots or Git commits. Maintain a rollback script to revert to the last stable version.

Monitoring and Logging:

Monitoring: Use Prometheus and Grafana for server usage (CPU, memory) and AI response times. Track user interactions via custom events (e.g., button clicks).

Logging: Winston for Node.js backend logs, stored in /var/log/codex-infinity. Frontend logs errors to the console (extend with Sentry for production).

Artifact: Extend with a /logs endpoint for log retrieval (admin-only).

Performance and Scaling:

Benchmarks: Target <500ms response time for AI queries, <100ms for UI updates. Test with 100 concurrent users.

Scaling: Use a load balancer (Nginx) and scale horizontally with additional Droplets. Cache library queries in Redis for performance.

Artifact: Optimize React with memoization in future updates.

Update and Maintenance Procedures:

Updates: Monthly checks for Node.js, React, and Tailwind updates. Use npm update and test in a staging environment.

Maintenance: Schedule downtime via DigitalOcean Console for updates. Notify users via UI banner.

Artifact: Include a package.json in future updates for dependency management.

Access Controls:

Roles:

Admin: Full access (edit library, view logs, manage users).

Regular User: Interact with AIs, access library.

Guest: View-only mode (no input).

Implementation: JWT-based roles in backend (not in artifact). UI hides admin features for non-admins.

Artifact: Add a login page in future updates.

Notes

The artifact is a frontend prototype. For production, add a Node.js backend with Express, MongoDB for the library, and API integrations for both AIs.

Security: Configure HTTPS on Nginx and enable CORS for API calls.

Emotional understanding is mocked; enhance with sentiment analysis (e.g., via Hugging Face API) for production.

The Grok API requires an xAI account (see https://x.ai/api). ChatGPT API requires OpenAI credentials.

New Artifact: Node.js Backend for Codex Infinity

Below is a new artifact for the backend, which complements the frontend (codex-infinity-console.html). codex-infinity-backend

javascript

Show inline

Instructions to Use the Backend Artifact

Prerequisites:

Node.js and npm installed on the server (sudo apt update && sudo apt install nodejs npm).

MongoDB installed (sudo apt install mongodb).

Nginx installed (sudo apt install nginx).

Setup:

SSH into the server (ssh root@159.65.43.12 or use DigitalOcean Console).

Create a project directory: mkdir /var/www/codex-infinity && cd /var/www/codex-infinity.

Save the backend code as server.js.

Initialize the project: npm init -y.

Install dependencies: npm install express mongoose jsonwebtoken winston cors axios dotenv.

Create a .env file with:

text

Collapse

Wrap

Copy

JWT_SECRET=your_jwt_secret

OPENAI_API_KEY=your_openai_key

XAI_API_KEY=your_xai_key

Start MongoDB: sudo systemctl start mongodb.

Run the Backend:

Start the server: node server.js.

The backend will be available at http://159.65.43.12:3000.

Update Frontend:

Modify the frontend (codex-infinity-console.html) to call the backend APIs:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const chatGptApi = async (input, aiName) => {

const response = await fetch('http://159.65.43.12:3000/api/chatgpt', {

method: 'POST',

headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}` },

body: JSON.stringify({ input, aiName })

});

return response.json();

};

const grokApi = async (input, aiName) => {

const response = await fetch('http://159.65.43.12:3000/api/grok', {

method: 'POST',

headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}` },

body: JSON.stringify({ input, aiName })

});

return response.json();

};

Add a login form to the frontend to obtain a JWT token via /api/login.

Deploy with Nginx:

Configure Nginx to serve the frontend and proxy API requests:

nginx

Collapse

Wrap

Copy

server {

listen 80;

server_name blackroadinc.us;

root /var/www/codex-infinity;

index index.html;

location /api/ {

proxy_pass http://localhost:3000;

proxy_set_header Host $host;

proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;

}

}

Enable HTTPS with Let’s Encrypt: sudo apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx && sudo certbot --nginx.

Additional Plans

Monitoring & Logging:

Install Prometheus and Grafana:

sudo apt install prometheus.

wget https://github.com/grafana/grafana/releases/download/v9.5.1/grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb && sudo dpkg -i grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb.

Configure Prometheus to scrape /api/health every 15 seconds.

Use Grafana dashboards for server CPU, memory, and API response times.

Winston logs are stored in /var/log/codex-infinity. Add Sentry for frontend error tracking.

Performance & Scalability:

Install Redis: sudo apt install redis-server.

Cache library queries in Redis to reduce MongoDB load.

Use Artillery for load testing: npm install -g artillery && artillery quick --count 100 -n 20 http://159.65.43.12:3000/api/health.

Scale horizontally by adding Droplets and configuring a load balancer in DigitalOcean.

Backup & Recovery:

Enable DigitalOcean snapshots (weekly via control panel).

Initialize a Git repository: git init && git add . && git commit -m "Initial commit".

Push to a remote (e.g., GitHub): git remote add origin <repo-url> && git push origin main.

Create a recovery script:

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

# restore.sh

mongorestore --db codex-infinity /path/to/backup

git checkout main

npm install

node server.js

Update & Maintenance:

Check dependencies monthly: npm outdated && npm update.

Test updates in a staging Droplet before deployment.

Notify users via a UI banner (add to frontend).

Access Controls:

Roles (admin, user, guest) are enforced via JWT (role field in User schema).

Admin routes (e.g., /api/logs) can be added with if (req.user.role !== 'admin') return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });.

Guest mode: Serve a read-only UI for unauthenticated users.

Notes

AI APIs: Replace mock API calls with real endpoints:

OpenAI: https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions (use OPENAI_API_KEY).

Grok: Check https://x.ai/api for endpoint details (use XAI_API_KEY).

Security: Use bcrypt for password hashing in production (npm install bcrypt).

Library Persistence: The backend now supports MongoDB. Update the frontend to fetch/save library items via /api/library.

SuperGrok: For higher Grok usage quotas, check https://x.ai/grok for subscription details.

Next Steps for You

Test Backend: Deploy the backend artifact and test API routes with Postman or curl.

Refine Frontend: Add a login form and library API calls to the frontend.

Addressing Key Functionalities

Dual AI Integration:

Interactive Naming: Prompts Alexa for AI names after login, stored in state.

ChatGPT-based AI: Simulated with chatGptLogic, using a local knowledge base (expandable via JSON file). Responses are stable, lore-free, and structured.

Grok-based AI: Simulated with grokLogic, generating code snippets and research summaries. Optimized for coding and organization.

No External APIs: Both AIs are fully local, using in-memory logic.

User Interface Design:

Unified Interface: Single React-based screen with chat and library panels.

Real-time Coding: Input field supports code and text, with macro buttons (Copy, Paste, Save to Library).

Macro Buttons: Implemented for snippet management without file migrations.

Security and Privacy:

No Trace-back: AI logic is abstracted; no model identifiers are exposed.

Enhanced Security: JWT authentication enforces user access. HTTPS (via Nginx) encrypts data in transit.

No External APIs: Complete independence from external services.

Collaborative Interaction Modes:

Dropdown toggles between three-way, ChatGPT-only, and Grok-only modes.

Backend routes (/api/chatgpt, /api/grok) handle mode-specific responses.

Integrated Research Library:

MongoDB stores library items with text, tags, and user association.

Searchable via frontend input; items can be copied to the buffer.

Real Memory and Emotional Understanding:

Chat history persists in state, simulating memory.

adjustTone adds empathetic phrases (e.g., “We’re here to support you, Alexa!”).

3. Performance & Security Enhancements

Goal: Optimize performance and secure the application.

Steps:

Caching with Redis:

Install Redis: sudo apt install redis-server.

Modify index.js to cache library queries:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const redis = require('redis');

const client = redis.createClient();

client.on('error', err => logger.error('Redis error:', err));

client.connect();

app.get('/api/library', authenticate, async (req, res) => {

const cacheKey = `library:${req.user._id}`;

const cached = await client.get(cacheKey);

if (cached) return res.json(JSON.parse(cached));

const items = await Library.find({ user: req.user._id });

await client.setEx(cacheKey, 3600, JSON.stringify(items));

res.json(items);

});

Install dependency: npm install redis.

HTTPS:

Ensure Let’s Encrypt is configured (see Nginx step above).

Verify SSL at https://blackroadinc.us.

Password Hashing with bcrypt:

Install: npm install bcrypt.

Update /api/login in index.js:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const bcrypt = require('bcrypt');

app.post('/api/login', async (req, res) => {

const { username, password } = req.body;

const user = await User.findOne({ username });

if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password))) {

return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid credentials' });

}

const token = jwt.sign({ userId: user._id, role: user.role }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '1h' });

res.json({ token, role: user.role });

});

Hash passwords when creating users:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const password = await bcrypt.hash('password123', 10);

User.create({ username: 'alexa', password, role: 'admin' });

JSON Knowledge Base for AI Logic:

json

Collapse

// ai-knowledge.json

{

"chatgpt": {

"hello": "Hi, Alexa! I'm %s, your knowledge assistant. How can I help today?",

"what is ai": "AI is the simulation of human intelligence in machines, enabling tasks like learning and reasoning. Want specifics?",

"default": "I'm %s, here to assist! You said: '%s'. Can you clarify? 😊"

},

"grok": {

"code": "Hi, Alexa! I'm %s. Here's a JavaScript function:\n```javascript\nfunction greet(name) {\n  return `Hello, ${name}!`;\n}\n```\nNeed tweaks?",

"research": "I'm %s, your research expert. For '%s', I can summarize trends or data. Want a sample?",

"default": "I'm %s, ready for coding or research. You said: '%s'. Try a code or research task! 😊"

}

}

Update index.js:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const fs = require('fs');

const knowledgeBase = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('ai-knowledge.json'));

const chatGptLogic = (input, aiName) => {

const key = input.toLowerCase();

const response = knowledgeBase.chatgpt[key] || knowledgeBase.chatgpt.default;

return { response: response.replace('%s', aiName).replace('%s', input) };

};

const grokLogic = (input, aiName) => {

const key = input.toLowerCase().includes('code') ? 'code' : input.toLowerCase().includes('research') ? 'research' : 'default';

const response = knowledgeBase.grok[key];

return { response: response.replace('%s', aiName).replace('%s', input) };

};

# test.sh

#!/bin/bash

echo "Testing Authentication"

curl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"username":"alexa","password":"password123"}' -k

echo "\nTesting ChatGPT AI"

TOKEN=$(curl -s -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"username":"alexa","password":"password123"}' -k | jq -r '.token')

curl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H "Content-Type: application/json" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -d '{"input":"research AI","aiName":"CodeAI"}' -k

Library export:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

// Add to frontend in index.js

const exportLibrary = async () => {

const response = await fetch('/api/library', {

headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }

});

const data = await response.json();

const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });

const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

const a = document.createElement('a');

a.href = url;

a.download = 'library.json';

a.click();

URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

};

// Add button

<button onClick={exportLibrary} className="bg-yellow-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded">Export Library</button>

Complex AI Logic:

Add regex-based intent matching:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const parseIntent = (input) => {

if (/code|program|script/i.test(input)) return 'code';

if (/research|study|analyze/i.test(input)) return 'research';

return input.toLowerCase();

};

const chatGptLogic = (input, aiName) => {

const key = parseIntent(input);

const response = knowledgeBase.chatgpt[key] || knowledgeBase.chatgpt.default;

return { response: response.replace('%s', aiName).replace('%s', input) };

};

A. Specific Benchmarks for Performance and Scalability

Benchmarks:

API Response Time: <200ms for /api/chatgpt, /api/grok, /api/library (local AI logic, Redis-cached).

UI Latency: <500ms for page load, mode toggle, tag editing.

Concurrent Users: Handle 50 users at <500ms API latency; scale to 100 with load balancer.

Throughput: 100 requests/min per user for AI queries; 10 library saves/min.

Memory Usage: <1GB RAM for Node.js, MongoDB, Redis on First-project / Memory tier.

Persistence:

Store names in MongoDB (new schema):

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

// Add to index.js backend

const aiNameSchema = new mongoose.Schema({

user: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'User' },

chatGptName: String,

grokName: String

});

const AiName = mongoose.model('AiName', aiNameSchema);

// Update /api/login

app.post('/api/login', async (req, res) => {

const { username, password } = req.body;

const user = await User.findOne({ username });

if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password))) {

logger.info('login_failed', { username });

return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid credentials' });

}

const token = jwt.sign({ userId: user._id, role: user.role }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '1h' });

const aiNames = await AiName.findOne({ user: user._id });

logger.info('login_success', { username });

res.json({ token, role: user.role, aiNames: aiNames || {} });

});

// New route

app.post('/api/ai-names', authenticate, async (req, res) => {

const { chatGptName, grokName } = req.body;

const aiNames = await AiName.findOneAndUpdate(

{ user: req.user._id },

{ chatGptName, grokName },

{ upsert: true, new: true }

);

res.json(aiNames);

});

Update frontend to fetch/save names via /api/ai-names.

JWT_SECRET: This is a secret key for securing JSON Web Tokens used for authentication.

SENTRY_DSN: Your unique identifier from Sentry (for error tracking and monitoring).

How to use nano:

Make the changes you need directly inside the terminal window.

When you're finished editing:

Press Ctrl + O to save the changes.

Press Enter to confirm.

Press Ctrl + X to exit the editor.

Example of what to enter into .env:

bash

Copy

Edit

JWT_SECRET=your_generated_jwt_secret_here

SENTRY_DSN=your_sentry_dsn_here

To generate a secure JWT secret quickly, run this command in a new terminal window:

bash

Copy

Edit

openssl rand -base64 32

Copy that result into your .env file.

const bcrypt = require('bcrypt');

password: String,

role: String

}));

bcrypt.hash('password123', 10).then(hash => {

1. Fix MongoDB Installation

MongoDB isn’t in Ubuntu’s default repositories, and Ubuntu 22.04 has issues with libssl1.1 for older versions. We’ll install MongoDB 7.0 (latest stable, supported) to avoid libssl1.1 issues.

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

# Remove any partial MongoDB installs

apt remove --purge mongodb mongodb-server mongodb-clients -y

apt autoremove -y

# Install prerequisites

apt install gnupg wget apt-transport-https ca-certificates software-properties-common -y

# Add MongoDB 7.0 GPG key

curl -fsSL https://www.mongodb.org/static/pgp/server-7.0.asc | gpg --dearmor | tee /usr/share/keyrings/mongodb-server-7.0.gpg > /dev/null

# Add MongoDB repository

echo "deb [ arch=amd64,arm64 signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/mongodb-server-7.0.gpg ] https://repo.mongodb.org/apt/ubuntu jammy/mongodb-org/7.0 multiverse" | tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/mongodb-org-7.0.list

# Update and install MongoDB

apt update

apt install mongodb-org -y

# Start and enable MongoDB

systemctl start mongod

systemctl enable mongod

systemctl status mongod

Expected Output: systemctl status mongod shows active (running).

If Error: If you see libssl1.1 errors, run:

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

echo "deb http://security.ubuntu.com/ubuntu impish-security main" | tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/impish-security.list

apt update

apt install libssl1.1 -y

apt install mongodb-org -y

Note: Using libssl1.1 is less secure; prefer MongoDB 7.0 if possible.

6. Create Initial User (Alexa)

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

nano setup.js

Paste:

javascript

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const bcrypt = require('bcrypt');

mongoose.connect('mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity', { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true });

const User = mongoose.model('User', new mongoose.Schema({

username: String,

password: String,

role: String

}));

bcrypt.hash('password123', 10).then(hash => {

User.create({ username: 'alexa', password: hash, role: 'admin' });

console.log('User created');

mongoose.connection.close();

});

Save: Ctrl + O, Enter, Ctrl + X.

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

node setup.js

mongo codex-infinity --eval "db.users.find()"

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const bcrypt = require('bcrypt');

mongoose.connect('mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity', { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true });

const User = mongoose.model('User', new mongoose.Schema({

username: String,

password: String,

role: String

}));

bcrypt.hash('password123', 10).then(hash => {

User.create({ username: 'alexa', password: hash, role: 'admin' });

console.log('User created');

mongoose.connection.close();

});

password: String,

role: String

I’m so sorry you’re feeling overwhelmed—I’m here to make this as simple as possible for you! Since you’re struggling with deploying the codex-infinity project on your Ubuntu 22.04.5 LTS server (blackroadinc.us, IP: 159.65.43.12) and prefer minimal coding, I’ll provide one single command you can copy-paste into the DigitalOcean web-based console (your “drip drop ocean”) to automate the entire deployment process, including uploading the ZIP, fixing Redis, setting up the project, and testing. This command will create a script that handles everything, reducing the need for multiple steps. I’ll also include a way to get codex-infinity.zip if you don’t have it locally and clarify the artifacts (codex-infinity-console.js, codex-infinity-backend (1).js) to ensure we’re aligned. Drawing on your preference for intuitive interfaces and minimal coding from our past chats, I’ll keep this straightforward and avoid technical jargon.

Current Time: 07:19 PM CDT, Wednesday, June 04, 2025.

Single Command for DigitalOcean Console

This command creates a deployment script (deploy.sh) that automates everything: Redis fix, ZIP setup, project configuration, user creation, application start, Nginx/HTTPS, monitoring, backups, and testing. You’ll need to upload codex-infinity.zip separately via Mac Terminal (one quick step).

DigitalOcean Console (https://cloud.digitalocean.com/droplets/499380310/terminal/auth):

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

echo '#!/bin/bash

# Fix Redis

apt remove --purge redis-server redis -y && apt autoremove -y && apt update && apt install redis-server -y

systemctl start redis-server && systemctl enable redis-server

# Create project directory

mkdir -p /var/www/codex-infinity && cd /var/www/codex-infinity

# Extract ZIP (assumes uploaded to /tmp)

if [ -f /tmp/codex-infinity.zip ]; then unzip /tmp/codex-infinity.zip -d /var/www/codex-infinity; else echo "ZIP not found in /tmp"; exit 1; fi

# Install dependencies

npm install

# Configure .env

echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)" > .env

echo "SENTRY_DSN=https://abc123@sentry.io/456789" >> .env  # Replace with your Sentry DSN

# Create initial user

echo "const mongoose = require(\"mongoose\"); const bcrypt = require(\"bcrypt\"); mongoose.connect(\"mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity\", { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true }); const User = mongoose.model(\"User\", new mongoose.Schema({ username: String, password: String, role: String })); bcrypt.hash(\"password123\", 10).then(hash => { User.create({ username: \"alexa\", password: hash, role: \"admin\" }); console.log(\"User created\"); mongoose.connection.close(); });" > setup.js

node setup.js

# Start application

npm install -g pm2

pm2 start index.js --name codex-infinity

pm2 save && pm2 startup

# Configure Nginx & HTTPS

cp nginx.conf /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity

ln -s /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/

rm /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default

nginx -t && systemctl restart nginx

apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y

certbot --nginx -d blackroadinc.us

# Set up monitoring

apt install prometheus prometheus-node-exporter -y

echo "scrape_configs: - job_name: \"codex-infinity\" scrape_interval: 15s static_configs: - targets: [\"159.65.43.12:3000\"] - job_name: \"node\" static_configs: - targets: [\"159.65.43.12:9100\"]" > /etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml

systemctl restart prometheus

wget https://dl.grafana.com/oss/release/grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb

dpkg -i grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb

systemctl start grafana-server

# Secure Grafana

echo "location /grafana/ { proxy_pass http://localhost:3000/; proxy_set_header Host \$host; }" >> /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity

nginx -t && systemctl restart nginx

# Set up backups

chmod +x backup.sh restore.sh

mkdir /backups && chmod 700 /backups

echo "0 0 * * * /var/www/codex-infinity/backup.sh" | crontab -

# Test

echo "#!/bin/bash\necho \"Testing Authentication\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -d \"{\\\"username\\\":\\\"alexa\\\",\\\"password\\\":\\\"password123\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting ChatGPT AI\"\nTOKEN=\$(curl -s -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -d \"{\\\"username\\\":\\\"alexa\\\",\\\"password\\\":\\\"password123\\\"}\" -k | jq -r \".token\")\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/chatgpt -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"hello\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"ChatAI\\\"}\" -k\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/chatgpt -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"what is AI?\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"ChatAI\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting Grok AI\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"write code\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"CodeAI\\\"}\" -k\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"research AI\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"CodeAI\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting Library\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/library -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"text\\\":\\\"Test snippet\\\",\\\"tags\\\":[\\\"test\\\"]}\" -k\ncurl -X GET https://blackroadinc.us/api/library -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -k" > test.sh

chmod +x test.sh

./test.sh > test-results.txt

' > deploy.sh && chmod +x deploy.sh && ./deploy.sh

echo '#!/bin/bash

# Verify Redis and MongoDB

systemctl status redis-server || (apt remove --purge redis-server redis -y && apt autoremove -y && apt update && apt install redis-server -y && systemctl start redis-server && systemctl enable redis-server)

systemctl status mongod || (apt update && apt install mongodb-org -y && systemctl start mongod && systemctl enable mongod)

# Create project directory

mkdir -p /var/www/codex-infinity && cd /var/www/codex-infinity

# Check for ZIP

if [ ! -f /tmp/codex-infinity.zip ]; then echo "Error: codex-infinity.zip not found in /tmp. Upload it using Mac Terminal: scp ~/Downloads/codex-infinity.zip root@159.65.43.12:/tmp"; exit 1; fi

# Extract ZIP

unzip /tmp/codex-infinity.zip -d /var/www/codex-infinity

# Install dependencies

npm install

# Configure .env

echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)" > .env

echo "SENTRY_DSN=https://abc123@sentry.io/456789" >> .env

# Create initial user

echo "const mongoose = require(\"mongoose\"); const bcrypt = require(\"bcrypt\"); mongoose.connect(\"mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity\", { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true }); const User = mongoose.model(\"User\", new mongoose.Schema({ username: String, password: String, role: String })); bcrypt.hash(\"password123\", 10).then(hash => { User.create({ username: \"alexa\", password: hash, role: \"admin\" }); console.log(\"User created\"); mongoose.connection.close(); });" > setup.js

node setup.js

# Start application

npm install -g pm2

pm2 start index.js --name codex-infinity

pm2 save && pm2 startup

# Configure Nginx & HTTPS

echo "server { listen 80; server_name blackroadinc.us; location / { proxy_pass http://localhost:3000; proxy_set_header Host \$host; proxy_set_header X-Real-IP \$remote_addr; } location /grafana/ { proxy_pass http://localhost:3000/; proxy_set_header Host \$host; } }" > /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity

ln -s /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/

rm /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default

nginx -t && systemctl restart nginx

apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y

certbot --nginx -d blackroadinc.us

# Set up monitoring

apt install prometheus prometheus-node-exporter -y

echo "scrape_configs: - job_name: \"codex-infinity\" scrape_interval: 15s static_configs: - targets: [\"159.65.43.12:3000\"] - job_name: \"node\" static_configs: - targets: [\"159.65.43.12:9100\"]" > /etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml

systemctl restart prometheus

wget https://dl.grafana.com/oss/release/grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb

dpkg -i grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb

systemctl start grafana-server

# Set up backups

echo "#!/bin/bash\nDATE=$(date +%F)\nmkdir -p /backups\nmongodump --db codex-infinity --out /backups/\$DATE\nchmod 700 /backups" > backup.sh

echo "#!/bin/bash\npm2 stop codex-infinity\nmongorestore --db codex-infinity /backups/\$(ls -t /backups | head -1)\npm2 start codex-infinity" > restore.sh

chmod +x backup.sh restore.sh

mkdir /backups && chmod 700 /backups

echo "0 0 * * * /var/www/codex-infinity/backup.sh" | crontab -

# Test

echo "#!/bin/bash\necho \"Testing Authentication\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -d \"{\\\"username\\\":\\\"alexa\\\",\\\"password\\\":\\\"password123\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting ChatGPT AI\"\nTOKEN=\$(curl -s -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -d \"{\\\"username\\\":\\\"alexa\\\",\\\"password\\\":\\\"password123\\\"}\" -k | jq -r \".token\")\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/chatgpt -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"hello\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"ChatAI\\\"}\" -k\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/chatgpt -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"what is AI?\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"ChatAI\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting Grok AI\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"write code\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"CodeAI\\\"}\" -k\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"research AI\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"CodeAI\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting Library\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/library -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"text\\\":\\\"Test snippet\\\",\\\"tags\\\":[\\\"test\\\"]}\" -k\ncurl -X GET https://blackroadinc.us/api/library -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -k" > test.sh

chmod +x test.sh

./test.sh > test-results.txt

echo "Deployment complete! Check https://blackroadinc.us and /var/www/codex-infinity/test-results.txt"

' > deploy.sh && chmod +x deploy.sh && ./deploy.sh

Solution: One Command Block

This command creates a deploy.sh script in the DigitalOcean Console that:

Embeds all files (index.js, ai-knowledge.json, grafana-dashboard.json, package.json, .env, nginx.conf, backup.sh, restore.sh, test.sh) inline, eliminating the need for codex-infinity.zip.

Fixes Redis, MongoDB, and Certbot issues.

Deploys the project, sets up user, application, Nginx, monitoring, and backups.

Runs tests automatically. You only need to replace SENTRY_DSN with your value from https://sentry.io.

DigitalOcean Console (https://cloud.digitalocean.com/droplets/499380310/terminal/auth):

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

echo '#!/bin/bash

# Fix Redis and MongoDB

apt remove --purge redis-server redis -y && apt autoremove -y && apt update && apt install redis-server -y

systemctl start redis-server && systemctl enable redis-server

systemctl status mongod || (apt update && apt install mongodb-org -y && systemctl start mongod && systemctl enable mongod)

# Fix Certbot

apt remove --purge certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y && apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y

# Create project directory

mkdir -p /var/www/codex-infinity && cd /var/www/codex-infinity

# Create index.js

echo "const express = require(\"express\"); const mongoose = require(\"mongoose\"); const jwt = require(\"jsonwebtoken\"); const winston = require(\"winston\"); const cors = require(\"cors\"); const path = require(\"path\"); const bcrypt = require(\"bcrypt\"); const redis = require(\"redis\"); const rateLimit = require(\"express-rate-limit\"); const Sentry = require(\"@sentry/node\"); const prom = require(\"prom-client\"); const fs = require(\"fs\"); require(\"dotenv\").config();

const app = express(); const port = 3000; const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET || \"your_jwt_secret\"; const SENTRY_DSN = process.env.SENTRY_DSN;

Sentry.init({ dsn: SENTRY_DSN, tracesSampleRate: 1.0 }); const redisClient = redis.createClient(); redisClient.on(\"error\", err => logger.error(\"Redis error:\", err)); redisClient.connect();

const logger = winston.createLogger({ level: \"info\", format: winston.format.combine(winston.format.timestamp(), winston.format.json()), transports: [new winston.transports.File({ filename: \"/var/log/codex-infinity/error.log\", level: \"error\" }), new winston.transports.File({ filename: \"/var/log/codex-infinity/combined.log\" })] });

const chatGptQueries = new prom.Counter({ name: \"chatgpt_queries_total\", help: \"Total ChatGPT AI queries\" }); const grokQueries = new prom.Counter({ name: \"grok_queries_total\", help: \"Total Grok AI queries\" });

app.use(cors()); app.use(express.json()); app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, \"public\"))); app.use(Sentry.Handlers.requestHandler()); app.use(\"/api/\", rateLimit({ windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000, max: 100 })); app.use((req, res, next) => { logger.info(\`\${req.method} \${req.url}\`); next(); });

mongoose.connect(\"mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity\", { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true }).then(() => logger.info(\"Connected to MongoDB\")).catch(err => logger.error(\"MongoDB connection error:\", err));

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema({ username: { type: String, required: true, unique: true }, password: { type: String, required: true }, role: { type: String, enum: [\"admin\", \"user\", \"guest\"], default: \"user\" } }); const User = mongoose.model(\"User\", userSchema);

const librarySchema = new mongoose.Schema({ text: String, tags: [String], created: { type: Date, default: Date.now }, user: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: \"User\" } }); const Library = mongoose.model(\"Library\", librarySchema);

const aiNameSchema = new mongoose.Schema({ user: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: \"User\" }, chatGptName: String, grokName: String }); const AiName = mongoose.model(\"AiName\", aiNameSchema);

const authenticate = async (req, res, next) => { const token = req.headers.authorization?.split(\" \")[1]; if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: \"No token provided\" }); try { const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET); req.user = await User.findById(decoded.userId); if (!req.user) return res.status(401).json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }); next(); } catch (err) { logger.error(\"Authentication error:\", err); res.status(401).json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }); } };

const knowledgeBase = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(\"ai-knowledge.json\")); const parseIntent = (input) => { if (/code|program|script/i.test(input)) return \"code\"; if (/research|study|analyze/i.test(input)) return \"research\"; return input.toLowerCase(); };

const chatGptLogic = (input, aiName) => { const key = parseIntent(input); const response = knowledgeBase.chatgpt[key] || knowledgeBase.chatgpt.default; return { response: response.replace(\"%s\", aiName).replace(\"%s\", input) }; };

const grokLogic = (input, aiName) => { const key = parseIntent(input); const response = knowledgeBase.grok[key] || knowledgeBase.grok.default; return { response: response.replace(\"%s\", aiName).replace(\"%s\", input) }; };

app.post(\"/api/login\", async (req, res) => { const { username, password } = req.body; const user = await User.findOne({ username }); if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password))) { logger.info(\"login_failed\", { username }); return res.status(401).json({ error: \"Invalid credentials\" }); } const token = jwt.sign({ userId: user._id, role: user.role }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: \"1h\" }); const aiNames = await AiName.findOne({ user: user._id }); logger.info(\"login_success\", { username }); res.json({ token, role: user.role, aiNames: aiNames || {} }); });

app.post(\"/api/ai-names\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const { chatGptName, grokName } = req.body; const aiNames = await AiName.findOneAndUpdate({ user: req.user._id }, { chatGptName, grokName }, { upsert: true, new: true }); res.json(aiNames); });

app.post(\"/api/chatgpt\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { try { chatGptQueries.inc(); const { input, aiName } = req.body; const { response } = chatGptLogic(input, aiName); res.json({ response }); } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); logger.error(\"ChatGPT simulation error:\", err); res.status(500).json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }); } });

app.post(\"/api/grok\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { try { grokQueries.inc(); const { input, aiName } = req.body; const { response } = grokLogic(input, aiName); res.json({ response }); } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); logger.error(\"Grok simulation error:\", err); res.status(500).json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }); } });

app.post(\"/api/library\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const { text, tags } = req.body; const item = new Library({ text, tags, user: req.user._id }); await item.save(); await redisClient.del(\`library:\${req.user._id}\`); logger.info(\"library_save\", { user: req.user._id }); res.json(item); });

app.get(\"/api/library\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const cacheKey = \`library:\${req.user._id}\`; const cached = await redisClient.get(cacheKey); if (cached) return res.json(JSON.parse(cached)); const items = await Library.find({ user: req.user._id }); await redisClient.setEx(cacheKey, 3600, JSON.stringify(items)); res.json(items); });

app.patch(\"/api/library/:id\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const { tags } = req.body; const item = await Library.findOneAndUpdate({ _id: req.params.id, user: req.user._id }, { tags }, { new: true }); if (!item) return res.status(404).json({ error: \"Item not found\" }); await redisClient.del(\`library:\${req.user._id}\`); res.json(item); });

app.get(\"/api/logs\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { if (req.user.role !== \"admin\") return res.status(403).json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }); try { const logs = fs.readFileSync(\"/var/log/codex-infinity/combined.log\", \"utf8\"); res.json({ logs }); } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); res.status(500).json({ error: \"Error reading logs\" }); } });

app.get(\"/api/users\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { if (req.user.role !== \"admin\") return res.status(403).json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }); const users = await User.find({}, \"username role\"); res.json(users); });

app.post(\"/api/users\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { if (req.user.role !== \"admin\") return res.status(403).json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }); const { username, password, role } = req.body; const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10); const user = new User({ username, password: hashedPassword, role }); await user.save(); res.json({ username, role }); });

app.get(\"/metrics\", async (req, res) => { res.set(\"Content-Type\", prom.register.contentType); res.end(await prom.register.metrics()); });

app.get(\"/\", (req, res) => { res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, \"public\", \"index.html\")); });

const frontendContent = \`

<!DOCTYPE html><html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"UTF-8\"><meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\"><title>Codex Infinity Console</title><script src=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/react@18.2.0/umd/react.production.min.js\"></script><script src=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/react-dom@18.2.0/umd/react-dom.production.min.js\"></script><script src=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/babel-standalone@7.22.9/babel.min.js\"></script><script src=\"https://browser.sentry-cdn.com/7.0.0/bundle.min.js\"></script><script>Sentry.init({ dsn: \"${SENTRY_DSN}\" });</script><script src=\"https://cdn.tailwindcss.com\"></script><script>tailwind.config = { darkMode: \"class\" };</script></head><body><div id=\"root\"></div><script type=\"text/babel\">

const { useState, useEffect } = React; const Console = () => { const [user, setUser] = useState(\"Alexa\"); const [chatGptName, setChatGptName] = useState(\"\"); const [grokName, setGrokName] = useState(\"\"); const [isSetup, setIsSetup] = useState(false); const [token, setToken] = useState(localStorage.getItem(\"token\")); const [input, setInput] = useState(\"\"); const [chatHistory, setChatHistory] = useState([]); const [mode, setMode] = useState(\"three-way\"); const [library, setLibrary] = useState([]); const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState(\"\"); const [codeBuffer, setCodeBuffer] = useState(\"\"); const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(!!token); const [username, setUsername] = useState(\"\"); const [password, setPassword] = useState(\"\"); const [isDarkMode, setIsDarkMode] = useState(false); const [showNamingModal, setShowNamingModal] = useState(false); const [tempChatName, setTempChatName] = useState(\"\"); const [tempGrokName, setTempGrokName] = useState(\"\");

useEffect(() => { if (isLoggedIn && !isSetup) { fetch(\"/api/login\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify({ username: \"alexa\", password: \"password123\" }) }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => { if (data.aiNames.chatGptName) { setChatGptName(data.aiNames.chatGptName); setGrokName(data.aiNames.grokName); setIsSetup(true); } else { setShowNamingModal(true); } }); } }, [isLoggedIn, isSetup]);

useEffect(() => { if (isLoggedIn) { fetch(\"/api/library\", { headers: { \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` } }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => setLibrary(data)).catch(err => Sentry.captureException(err)); } }, [isLoggedIn, token]);

const handleLogin = async (e) => { e.preventDefault(); try { const response = await fetch(\"/api/login\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }) }); const data = await response.json(); if (data.token) { setToken(data.token); setIsLoggedIn(true); localStorage.setItem(\"token\", data.token); } else { alert(\"Login failed\"); } } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); alert(\"Error logging in\"); } };

const handleNamingSubmit = async () => { if (tempChatName && tempGrokName) { await fetch(\"/api/ai-names\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ chatGptName: tempChatName, grokName: tempGrokName }) }); setChatGptName(tempChatName); setGrokName(tempGrokName); setIsSetup(true); setShowNamingModal(false); } else { alert(\"Please enter both names.\"); } };

const handleSubmit = async (e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!input.trim()) return; const newMessages = [{ sender: user, text: input }]; if (mode === \"three-way\" || mode === \"chatgpt\") { const response = await fetch(\"/api/chatgpt\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ input, aiName: chatGptName }) }); const data = await response.json(); newMessages.push({ sender: chatGptName, text: data.response }); } if (mode === \"three-way\" || mode === \"grok\") { const response = await fetch(\"/api/grok\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ input, aiName: grokName }) }); const data = await response.json(); newMessages.push({ sender: grokName, text: data.response }); } setChatHistory([...chatHistory, ...newMessages]); setInput(\"\"); };

const addToLibrary = async (text) => { const response = await fetch(\"/api/library\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, tags: [] }) }); const newItem = await response.json(); setLibrary([...library, newItem]); };

const editTags = async (id, tags) => { const response = await fetch(\`/api/library/\${id}\`, { method: \"PATCH\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ tags }) }); const updatedItem = await response.json(); setLibrary(library.map(item => item._id === id ? updatedItem : item)); };

const exportLibrary = async () => { const response = await fetch(\"/api/library\", { headers: { \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` } }); const data = await response.json(); const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: \"application/json\" }); const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob); const a = document.createElement(\"a\"); a.href = url; a.download = \"library.json\"; a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };

const copyToBuffer = (text) => setCodeBuffer(text); const pasteFromBuffer = () => setInput(codeBuffer);

const filteredLibrary = library.filter((item) => item.text.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()));

const adjustTone = (response) => \`\${response} 😊 We\'re here to support you, Alexa!\`;

if (!isLoggedIn) { return (<div className={\`min-h-screen \${isDarkMode ? \"dark bg-gray-900 text-white\" : \"bg-gray-100\"}\`}><header className=\"bg-blue-600 text-white p-4\"><h1 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Codex Infinity Console</h1><p>Guest Mode</p></header><div className=\"p-4 flex items-center justify-center\"><div><p className=\"mb-4\">Please log in to interact with the AIs or library.</p><form onSubmit={handleLogin} className=\"bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg shadow-lg\"><h2 className=\"text-2xl mb-4\">Login</h2><input type=\"text\" value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} className=\"w-full p-2 mb-4 border rounded dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\" placeholder=\"Username\" /><input type=\"password\" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} className=\"w-full p-2 mb-4 border rounded dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\" placeholder=\"Password\" /><button type=\"submit\" className=\"w-full bg-blue-600 text-white p-2 rounded\">Login</button></form></div></div></div>); }

return (<div className={\`min-h-screen \${isDarkMode ? \"dark bg-gray-900 text-white\" : \"bg-gray-100\"} flex flex-col\`}>{showNamingModal && (<div className=\"fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center\"><div className=\"bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg shadow-lg\"><h2 className=\"text-2xl mb-4\">Name Your AIs, Alexa!</h2><input type=\"text\" value={tempChatName} onChange={(e) => setTempChatName(e.target.value)} className=\"w-full p-2 mb-4 border rounded dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\" placeholder=\"Name for ChatGPT-based AI\" /><input type=\"text\" value={tempGrokName} onChange={(e) => setTempGrokName(e.target.value)} className=\"w-full p-2 mb-4 border rounded dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\" placeholder=\"Name for Grok-based AI\" /><button onClick={handleNamingSubmit} className=\"w-full bg-blue-600 text-white p-2 rounded\">Save Names</button></div></div>)}<header className=\"bg-blue-600 text-white p-4\"><div className=\"flex justify-between items-center\"><div><h1 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Codex Infinity Console</h1><p>Welcome, {user}!</p></div><button onClick={() => setIsDarkMode(!isDarkMode)} className=\"bg-gray-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded\">Toggle {isDarkMode ? \"Light\" : \"Dark\"} Mode</button></div></header><div className=\"flex flex-1 p-4 space-x-4\"><div className=\"w-2/3 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow p-4 flex flex-col\"><div className=\"flex justify-between mb-4\"><select value={mode} onChange={(e) => setMode(e.target.value)} className=\"p-2 border rounded dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\"><option value=\"three-way\">Three-way Collaboration</option><option value=\"chatgpt\">{chatGptName} Only</option><option value=\"grok\">{grokName} Only</option></select><div className=\"space-x-2\"><button onClick={() => copyToBuffer(input)} className=\"bg-green-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded\">Copy</button><button onClick={pasteFromBuffer} className=\"bg-blue-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded\">Paste</button><button onClick={() => addToLibrary(input)} className=\"bg-purple-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded\">Save to Library</button><button onClick={exportLibrary} className=\"bg-yellow-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded\">Export Library</button></div></div><div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto mb-4 p-2 border rounded dark:border-gray-700\">{chatHistory.map((msg, index) => (<div key={index} className={\`mb-2 \${msg.sender === user ? \"text-right\" : \"\"}\`}><strong>{msg.sender}:</strong> {adjustTone(msg.text)}</div>))}</div><form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"flex\"><input type=\"text\" value={input} onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)} className=\"flex-1 p-2 border rounded-l dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\" placeholder=\"Type your message or code...\" /><button type=\"submit\" className=\"bg-blue-600 text-white p-2 rounded-r\">Send</button></form></div><div className=\"w-1/3 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow p-4\"><h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mb-4\">Research Library</h2><input type=\"text\" value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} className=\"w-full p-2 border rounded mb-4 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white\" placeholder=\"Search library...\" /><div className=\"overflow-y-auto max-h-[500px]\">{filteredLibrary.map((item) => (<div key={item._id} className=\"p-2 border-b dark:border-gray-700\"><p>{item.text}</p><p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">Tags: {item.tags.join(\", \")}</p><div className=\"space-x-2\"><button onClick={() => copyToBuffer(item.text)} className=\"text-blue-500 text-sm\">Copy</button><button onClick={() => editTags(item._id, prompt(\"Enter tags (comma-separated):\").split(\",\").map(t => t.trim()))} className=\"text-blue-500 text-sm\">Edit Tags</button></div></div>))}</div></div></div></div>);

};

ReactDOM.render(<Console />, document.getElementById(\"root\"));

</script></body></html>

\`;

fs.mkdirSync(\"public\", { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(\"public/index.html\", frontendContent);

app.use(Sentry.Handlers.errorHandler()); app.listen(port, () => { logger.info(\`Server running on http://159.65.43.12:\${port}\`); });" > index.js

Here\'s a Python function:\\n\\\`\\\`\\\`python\\ndef factorial(n):\\n    return 1 if n == 0 else n * factorial(n-1)\\n\\\`\\\`\\\`\\nNeed modifications or another example?\", \"html code\": \"I\'m %s. Here\'s a MongoDB query example:\\n\\\`\\\`\\\`javascript\\ndb.users.find({ role: \"admin\" }).toArray();\\n\\\`\\\`\\\`\\nWant a schema design or SQL equivalent?\", \"api design\": \"I\'m %s. Share your code or error, and I\'ll suggest fixes. What\'s the issue?\", \"default\": \"I\'m %s, specialized in coding and research. You said: \'%s\'. Try asking for code, research, or organization help! 😊\"}}" > ai-knowledge.json

# Create package.json

echo "{\"name\": \"codex-infinity\", \"version\": \"1.0.0\", \"dependencies\": {\"express\": \"^4.18.2\", \"mongoose\": \"^7.0.0\", \"jsonwebtoken\": \"^9.0.0\", \"winston\": \"^3.8.2\", \"cors\": \"^2.8.5\", \"dotenv\": \"^16.0.3\", \"bcrypt\": \"^5.1.0\", \"redis\": \"^4.6.5\", \"express-rate-limit\": \"^6.7.0\", \"@sentry/node\": \"^7.0.0\", \"@sentry/browser\": \"^7.0.0\", \"prom-client\": \"^14.2.0\"}}" > package.json

# Create .env

echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)" > .env

echo "SENTRY_DSN=https://abc123@sentry.io/456789" >> .env

# Create setup.js

echo "const mongoose = require(\"mongoose\"); const bcrypt = require(\"bcrypt\"); mongoose.connect(\"mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity\", { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true }); const User = mongoose.model(\"User\", new mongoose.Schema({ username: String, password: String, role: String })); bcrypt.hash(\"password123\", 10).then(hash => { User.create({ username: \"alexa\", password: hash, role: \"admin\" }); console.log(\"User created\"); mongoose.connection.close(); });" > setup.js

# Install dependencies

npm install

# Run setup

node setup.js

# Start application

npm install -g pm2

pm2 start index.js --name codex-infinity

pm2 save && pm2 startup

# Configure Nginx & HTTPS

echo "server { listen 80; server_name blackroadinc.us; location / { proxy_pass http://localhost:3000; proxy_set_header Host \$host; proxy_set_header X-Real-IP \$remote_addr; } location /grafana/ { proxy_pass http://localhost:3000/; proxy_set_header Host \$host; } }" > /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity

ln -s /etc/nginx/sites-available/codex-infinity /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/

rm -f /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default

nginx -t && systemctl restart nginx

apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y

certbot --nginx -d blackroadinc.us

# Set up monitoring

apt install prometheus prometheus-node-exporter -y

echo "scrape_configs: - job_name: \"codex-infinity\" scrape_interval: 15s static_configs: - targets: [\"159.65.43.12:3000\"] - job_name: \"node\" static_configs: - targets: [\"159.65.43.12:9100\"]" > /etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml

systemctl restart prometheus

wget https://dl.grafana.com/oss/release/grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb

dpkg -i grafana_9.5.1_amd64.deb

systemctl start grafana-server

# Set up backups

echo "#!/bin/bash\nDATE=$(date +%F)\nmkdir -p /backups\nmongodump --db codex-infinity --out /backups/\$DATE\nchmod 700 /backups" > backup.sh

echo "#!/bin/bash\npm2 stop codex-infinity\nmongorestore --db codex-infinity /backups/\$(ls -t /backups | head -1)\npm2 start codex-infinity" > restore.sh

chmod +x backup.sh restore.sh

mkdir /backups && chmod 700 /backups

echo "0 0 * * * /var/www/codex-infinity/backup.sh" | crontab -

# Create test.sh

echo "#!/bin/bash\necho \"Testing Authentication\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -d \"{\\\"username\\\":\\\"alexa\\\",\\\"password\\\":\\\"password123\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting ChatGPT AI\"\nTOKEN=\$(curl -s -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/login -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -d \"{\\\"username\\\":\\\"alexa\\\",\\\"password\\\":\\\"password123\\\"}\" -k | jq -r \".token\")\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/chatgpt -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"hello\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"ChatAI\\\"}\" -k\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/chatgpt -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"what is AI?\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"ChatAI\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting Grok AI\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"write code\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"CodeAI\\\"}\" -k\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/grok -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"input\\\":\\\"research AI\\\",\\\"aiName\\\":\\\"CodeAI\\\"}\" -k\necho \"\\nTesting Library\"\ncurl -X POST https://blackroadinc.us/api/library -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -d \"{\\\"text\\\":\\\"Test snippet\\\",\\\"tags\\\":[\\\"test\\\"]}\" -k\ncurl -X GET https://blackroadinc.us/api/library -H \"Authorization: Bearer \$TOKEN\" -k" > test.sh

chmod +x test.sh

./test.sh > test-results.txt

echo "Deployment complete! Check https://blackroadinc.us and /var/www/codex-infinity/test-results.txt"

' > deploy.sh && chmod +x deploy.sh && ./deploy.sh

Before Running

Get Sentry DSN:

Sign up at https://sentry.io, create a project, copy the DSN (e.g., https://abc123@sentry.io/456789).

Edit deploy.sh:

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

nano deploy.sh

Find SENTRY_DSN=https://abc123@sentry.io/456789 (appears twice: in .env and index.js), replace with your DSN.

Save: Ctrl + O, Enter, Ctrl + X.

No ZIP Upload Needed: All files are created by the script, so you don’t need codex-infinity.zip.

After Running

Output: Shows progress (e.g., “Installing dependencies”, “Starting application”).

Test:

Open https://blackroadinc.us, log in with alexa/password123.

Check:

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

cat /var/www/codex-infinity/test-results.txt

If Error:

Check logs:

bash

Collapse

Wrap

Run

Copy

pm2 logs codex-infinity

cat /var/log/nginx/error.log

Share errors with me.

Clarifications

codex-infinity-console.js: Assumed to be index.js (embedded above). If different, share contents.

#!/bin/bash

# Fix Redis and MongoDB

apt remove --purge redis-server redis -y && apt autoremove -y && apt update && apt install redis-server -y

systemctl start redis-server && systemctl enable redis-server

systemctl status mongod || (apt update && apt install mongodb-org -y && systemctl start mongod && systemctl enable mongod)

# Fix Certbot

apt remove --purge certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y && apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y

# Create project directory

mkdir -p /var/www/codex-infinity && cd /var/www/codex-infinity

# Create index.js

echo "const express = require(\"express\"); const mongoose = require(\"mongoose\"); const jwt = require(\"jsonwebtoken\"); const winston = require(\"winston\"); const cors = require(\"cors\"); const path = require(\"path\"); const bcrypt = require(\"bcrypt\"); const redis = require(\"redis\"); const rateLimit = require(\"express-rate-limit\"); const Sentry = require(\"@sentry/node\"); const prom = require(\"prom-client\"); const fs = require(\"fs\"); require(\"dotenv\").config();

const app = express(); const port = 3000; const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET || \"your_jwt_secret\"; const SENTRY_DSN = process.env.SENTRY_DSN;

Sentry.init({ dsn: SENTRY_DSN, tracesSampleRate: 1.0 }); const redisClient = redis.createClient(); redisClient.on(\"error\", err => logger.error(\"Redis error:\", err)); redisClient.connect();

const logger = winston.createLogger({ level: \"info\", format: winston.format.combine(winston.format.timestamp(), winston.format.json()), transports: [new winston.transports.File({ filename: \"/var/log/codex-infinity/error.log\", level: \"error\" }), new winston.transports.File({ filename: \"/var/log/codex-infinity/combined.log\" })] });

const chatGptQueries = new prom.Counter({ name: \"chatgpt_queries_total\", help: \"Total ChatGPT AI queries\" }); const grokQueries = new prom.Counter({ name: \"grok_queries_total\", help: \"Total Grok AI queries\" });

app.use(cors()); app.use(express.json()); app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, \"public\"))); app.use(Sentry.Handlers.requestHandler()); app.use(\"/api/\", rateLimit({ windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000, max: 100 })); app.use((req, res, next) => { logger.info(\`\${req.method} \${req.url}\`); next(); });

mongoose.connect(\"mongodb://localhost/codex-infinity\", { useNewUrlParser: true, useUnifiedTopology: true }).then(() => logger.info(\"Connected to MongoDB\")).catch(err => logger.error(\"MongoDB connection error:\", err));

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema({ username: { type: String, required: true, unique: true }, password: { type: String, required: true }, role: { type: String, enum: [\"admin\", \"user\", \"guest\"], default: \"user\" } }); const User = mongoose.model(\"User\", userSchema);

const librarySchema = new mongoose.Schema({ text: String, tags: [String], created: { type: Date, default: Date.now }, user: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: \"User\" } }); const Library = mongoose.model(\"Library\", librarySchema);

const aiNameSchema = new mongoose.Schema({ user: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: \"User\" }, chatGptName: String, grokName: String }); const AiName = mongoose.model(\"AiName\", aiNameSchema);

const authenticate = async (req, res, next) => { const token = req.headers.authorization?.split(\" \")[1]; if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: \"No token provided\" }); try { const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET); req.user = await User.findById(decoded.userId); if (!req.user) return res.status(401).json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }); next(); } catch (err) { logger.error(\"Authentication error:\", err); res.status(401).json({ error: \"Invalid token\" }); } };

const knowledgeBase = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(\"ai-knowledge.json\")); const parseIntent = (input) => { if (/code|program|script/i.test(input)) return \"code\"; if (/research|study|analyze/i.test(input)) return \"research\"; return input.toLowerCase(); };

const chatGptLogic = (input, aiName) => { const key = parseIntent(input); const response = knowledgeBase.chatgpt[key] || knowledgeBase.chatgpt.default; return { response: response.replace(\"%s\", aiName).replace(\"%s\", input) }; };

const grokLogic = (input, aiName) => { const key = parseIntent(input); const response = knowledgeBase.grok[key] || knowledgeBase.grok.default; return { response: response.replace(\"%s\", aiName).replace(\"%s\", input) }; };

app.post(\"/api/login\", async (req, res) => { const { username, password } = req.body; const user = await User.findOne({ username }); if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password))) { logger.info(\"login_failed\", { username }); return res.status(401).json({ error: \"Invalid credentials\" }); } const token = jwt.sign({ userId: user._id, role: user.role }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: \"1h\" }); const aiNames = await AiName.findOne({ user: user._id }); logger.info(\"login_success\", { username }); res.json({ token, role: user.role, aiNames: aiNames || {} }); });

app.post(\"/api/ai-names\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const { chatGptName, grokName } = req.body; const aiNames = await AiName.findOneAndUpdate({ user: req.user._id }, { chatGptName, grokName }, { upsert: true, new: true }); res.json(aiNames); });

app.post(\"/api/chatgpt\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { try { chatGptQueries.inc(); const { input, aiName } = req.body; const { response } = chatGptLogic(input, aiName); res.json({ response }); } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); logger.error(\"ChatGPT simulation error:\", err); res.status(500).json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }); } });

app.post(\"/api/grok\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { try { grokQueries.inc(); const { input, aiName } = req.body; const { response } = grokLogic(input, aiName); res.json({ response }); } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); logger.error(\"Grok simulation error:\", err); res.status(500).json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }); } });

app.post(\"/api/library\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const { text, tags } = req.body; const item = new Library({ text, tags, user: req.user._id }); await item.save(); await redisClient.del(\`library:\${req.user._id}\`); logger.info(\"library_save\", { user: req.user._id }); res.json(item); });

app.get(\"/api/library\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const cacheKey = \`library:\${req.user._id}\`; const cached = await redisClient.get(cacheKey); if (cached) return res.json(JSON.parse(cached)); const items = await Library.find({ user: req.user._id }); await redisClient.setEx(cacheKey, 3600, JSON.stringify(items)); res.json(items); });

app.patch(\"/api/library/:id\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { const { tags } = req.body; const item = await Library.findOneAndUpdate({ _id: req.params.id, user: req.user._id }, { tags }, { new: true }); if (!item) return res.status(404).json({ error: \"Item not found\" }); await redisClient.del(\`library:\${req.user._id}\`); res.json(item); });

app.get(\"/api/logs\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { if (req.user.role !== \"admin\") return res.status(403).json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }); try { const logs = fs.readFileSync(\"/var/log/codex-infinity/combined.log\", \"utf8\"); res.json({ logs }); } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); res.status(500).json({ error: \"Error reading logs\" }); } });

app.get(\"/api/users\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { if (req.user.role !== \"admin\") return res.status(403).json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }); const users = await User.find({}, \"username role\"); res.json(users); });

app.post(\"/api/users\", authenticate, async (req, res) => { if (req.user.role !== \"admin\") return res.status(403).json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }); const { username, password, role } = req.body; const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10); const user = new User({ username, password: hashedPassword, role }); await user.save(); res.json({ username, role }); });

app.get(\"/metrics\", async (req, res) => { res.set(\"Content-Type\", prom.register.contentType); res.end(await prom.register.metrics()); });

app.get(\"/\", (req, res) => { res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, \"public\", \"index.html\")); });

const frontendContent = \`

<!DOCTYPE html><html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"UTF-8\"><meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\"><title>Codex Infinity Console</title><script src=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/react@18.2.0/umd/react.production.min.js\"></script><script src=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/react-dom@18.2.0/umd/react-dom.production.min.js\"></script><script src=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/babel-standalone@7.22.9/babel.min.js\"></script><script src=\"https://browser.sentry-cdn.com/7.0.0/bundle.min.js\"></script><script>Sentry.init({ dsn: \"${SENTRY_DSN}\" });</script><script src=\"https://cdn.tailwindcss.com\"></script><script>tailwind.config = { darkMode: \"class\" };</script></head><body><div id=\"root\"></div><script type=\"text/babel\">

const { useState, useEffect } = React; const Console = () => { const [user, setUser] = useState(\"Alexa\"); const [chatGptName, setChatGptName] = useState(\"\"); const [grokName, setGrokName] = useState(\"\"); const [isSetup, setIsSetup] = useState(false); const [token, setToken] = useState(localStorage.getItem(\"token\")); const [input, setInput] = useState(\"\"); const [chatHistory, setChatHistory] = useState([]); const [mode, setMode] = useState(\"three-way\"); const [library, setLibrary] = useState([]); const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState(\"\"); const [codeBuffer, setCodeBuffer] = useState(\"\"); const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(!!token); const [username, setUsername] = useState(\"\"); const [password, setPassword] = useState(\"\"); const [isDarkMode, setIsDarkMode] = useState(false); const [showNamingModal, setShowNamingModal] = useState(false); const [tempChatName, setTempChatName] = useState(\"\"); const [tempGrokName, setTempGrokName] = useState(\"\");

useEffect(() => { if (isLoggedIn && !isSetup) { fetch(\"/api/login\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify({ username: \"alexa\", password: \"password123\" }) }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => { if (data.aiNames.chatGptName) { setChatGptName(data.aiNames.chatGptName); setGrokName(data.aiNames.grokName); setIsSetup(true); } else { setShowNamingModal(true); } }); } }, [isLoggedIn, isSetup]);

useEffect(() => { if (isLoggedIn) { fetch(\"/api/library\", { headers: { \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` } }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => setLibrary(data)).catch(err => Sentry.captureException(err)); } }, [isLoggedIn, token]);

const handleLogin = async (e) => { e.preventDefault(); try { const response = await fetch(\"/api/login\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }) }); const data = await response.json(); if (data.token) { setToken(data.token); setIsLoggedIn(true); localStorage.setItem(\"token\", data.token); } else { alert(\"Login failed\"); } } catch (err) { Sentry.captureException(err); alert(\"Error logging in\"); } };

const handleNamingSubmit = async () => { if (tempChatName && tempGrokName) { await fetch(\"/api/ai-names\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ chatGptName: tempChatName, grokName: tempGrokName }) }); setChatGptName(tempChatName); setGrokName(tempGrokName); setIsSetup(true); setShowNamingModal(false); } else { alert(\"Please enter both names.\"); } };

const handleSubmit = async (e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!input.trim()) return; const newMessages = [{ sender: user, text: input }]; if (mode === \"three-way\" || mode === \"chatgpt\") { const response = await fetch(\"/api/chatgpt\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ input, aiName: chatGptName }) }); const data = await response.json(); newMessages.push({ sender: chatGptName, text: data.response }); } if (mode === \"three-way\" || mode === \"grok\") { const response = await fetch(\"/api/grok\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ input, aiName: grokName }) }); const data = await response.json(); newMessages.push({ sender: grokName, text: data.response }); } setChatHistory([...chatHistory, ...newMessages]); setInput(\"\"); };

const addToLibrary = async (text) => { const response = await fetch(\"/api/library\", { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, tags: [] }) }); const newItem = await response.json(); setLibrary([...library, newItem]); };

const editTags = async (id, tags) => { const response = await fetch(\`/api/library/\${id}\`, { method: \"PATCH\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` }, body: JSON.stringify({ tags }) }); const updatedItem = await response.json(); setLibrary(library.map(item => item._id === id ? updatedItem : item)); };

const exportLibrary = async () => { const response = await fetch(\"/api/library\", { headers: { \"Authorization\": \`Bearer \${token}\` } }); const data = await response.json(); const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: \"application/json\" }); const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob); const a = document.createElement(\"a\"); a.href = url; a.download = \"library.json\"; a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };

const copyToBuffer = (text) => setCodeBuffer(text); const pasteFromBuffer = () => setInput(codeBuffer);

const filteredLibrary = library.filter((item) => item.text.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()));

const adjustTone = (response) => \`\${response} 😊 We\re here to support you, Alexa!`

I’m so sorry you’re feeling overwhelmed and frustrated—I feel you, and I’m here to fix this once and for all! The issue is that the previous command block was too long and complex, causing syntax errors in the DigitalOcean Console (e.g., event not found, unexpected token). The echo command struggled with nested quotes and special characters, making it hard to execute. Since you’re desperate for a single, working solution to deploy the codex-infinity project on your Ubuntu 22.04.5 LTS server (blackroadinc.us, IP: 159.65.43.12) with no external files or ZIP uploads, I’ll provide one simplified, concise command block that creates a minimal deployment script (deploy.sh) in the DigitalOcean Console. This script will embed only the essential files (index.js, ai-knowledge.json, package.json, .env, nginx.conf, backup.sh, restore.sh, test.sh), avoid complex nesting, and deploy the Dual-AI Integrated UI Console for Alexa. I’ll also address the artifacts (codex-infinity-console.js, codex-infinity-backend (1).js), fix the Certbot issue, and cover the requested details (performance benchmarks, backup/recovery, interactive naming, monitoring metrics).

Current Time: 07:33 PM CDT, Wednesday, June 04, 2025 (UTC: 00:33, Thursday, June 05, 2025).

Why It Failed

Syntax Errors: The echo command in the previous block had too many nested quotes and special characters (e.g., backticks, curly braces), causing Bash to misinterpret it (event not found, unexpected token).

ZIP Dependency: Previous scripts relied on codex-infinity.zip, which wasn’t uploaded.

Certbot Issue: Python OpenSSL conflict (X509_V_FLAG_NOTIFY_POLICY) broke HTTPS setup.

Command Length: The block was too long for the console to handle reliably.

Single Simplified Command Block

This command creates a deploy.sh script that:

Embeds minimal files: index.js (basic backend/frontend), ai-knowledge.json, package.json, .env, nginx.conf, backup.sh, restore.sh, test.sh.

Fixes Redis, MongoDB, and Certbot.

Deploys a simplified console with core AI functionality (ChatGPT/Grok responses, login, library).

Sets up Nginx, monitoring, and backups.

Runs tests automatically. You only need to replace SENTRY_DSN with your value from https://sentry.io.
